--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   1- 10 A10    ---     ID           Short ID of a star, Paper I

  20- 24 F5.2   [-]     logeps       ?="" Log number abundance of Ion
  26- 26 A1     ---   l_XFe          Limit flag for XFe
  28- 32 F5.2   [-]     XFe          ?="" Abundance ratio of [X/Fe]
  34- 38 F5.2   ---     sigma-Teff   ?="" Abundance error associated with Teff uncertainty
  40- 44 F5.2   ---     sigma-logg   ?="" Abundance error associated with logg uncertainty
  46- 50 F5.2   ---     sigma-FeH    ?="" Abundance error associated with [Fe/H] uncertainty

  63- 66 F4.2   ---     sigma-Total  ?="" Total abundance error
  68- 70 I3     ---     Nlines       Number of adopted lines
